# Flag names Roblox publishes for the Android client, as an inventory.
#
# Names only, with which application each appears under. No values: values
# change under Roblox continuously and a snapshot of them here would be a
# stale claim about live configuration within days, which is the kind of thing
# this repository keeps retracting. Fetch them when you need them.
#
#   curl -s https://clientsettingscdn.roblox.com/v2/settings/application/GoogleAndroidApp
#   curl -s https://clientsettingscdn.roblox.com/v2/settings/application/AndroidApp
#
# This is Roblox's own published client configuration over a public endpoint --
# the same document the engine fetches for itself at t~1.6-2.3 s, which is why
# DF* overrides last about two seconds (flag-init.md section 47). It is not
# derived from the binary and nothing here was disassembled.
#
# It exists because `native-flag-names.txt` holds 139 names and a search for a
# TaskScheduler lever across it returned nothing -- not because no such flag
# exists, but because the inventory was three orders of magnitude short.
#
# Recall which family is durable before reaching for one of these: FFlag, FInt
# and FString are read once at startup and hold; DFFlag, DFInt and DFString are
# re-read and reverted about two seconds in.
#
# Captured 2026-08-21.
